	trigger_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.combat.location }
		scope:combat_side.combat.location = {
			is_raided = yes
			is_occupied = no
		}
	}
}


# BATCH 205 - HOW MUCH OF A PLACE THIS PLACE IS.
#
# CENSUS. `is_county_capital`, `num_buildings`, `combined_building_level`,
# `building_slots`, `free_building_slots` and `has_free_building_slot` all
# return zero across the whole mod. Batch 204 at :9443 asked what has been
# HAPPENING to the ground - taken, or being stripped. This one asks HOW MUCH IS
# ON it, which is a different fact entirely: a burnt-out county seat has been
# happening to for months and is still a county seat, and an empty hillside can
# be perfectly peaceful and still have nothing on it at all.
#
# IT DOES NOT BELONG IN bc_ground_triggers.txt for the same reason batch 204
# does not. That file holds the six terrain bands and nothing else, validate
# counts its `agot_is_*_terrain` mentions against the 23 base helpers, and
# neither gate below mentions a terrain.
#
# PRECEDENT, BOTH KEYS AGOT-ORIGINAL. `num_buildings` is read in files of
# AGOT's own making at `common/script_values/00_agot_war_values.txt:94` as
# `any_county_province = { num_buildings >= 2 }`, at
# `events/agot_filler/00_agot_dragon_filler_events.txt:7802` and `:7836` as
# `num_buildings > 1`, and as an `order_by` at
# `common/casus_belli_types/00_agot_nights_watch_wars.txt:282`. AGOT writes
# `is_county_capital = yes` throughout `common/buildings/
# 00_agot_standard_economy_buildings.txt` from `:710`. Vanilla has both as
# well: `common/buildings/00_standard_economy_buildings.txt:3917` and `:3926`
# compare `num_buildings < 5` and `num_buildings >= 8`, and the same file reads
# `is_county_capital` in both directions from `:77`. triggers.log gives
# num_buildings as a province value and is_county_capital as province yes/no.
#
# THE THRESHOLDS COME FROM THOSE FILES. AGOT's own bar for a province anybody
# has built on is two, and vanilla's own bar for a province that is still thin
# is `num_buildings < 5`. The first gate asks for five or more, which is
# vanilla's line read from the other side, and the second asks for none at all,
# which is the floor and needs no calibrating.
#
# DISJOINT BY CONSTRUCTION on both clauses, the fostering pair's arrangement at
# :2490-2510: five or more buildings against none, and county seat against not.
# No province can satisfy both halves of either pair.
#
# NOT THE BATTLE-SIZE, OCCUPATION, FORTIFICATION OR HOLDING ROWS. `:185` and
# the size bucket at :615 count men on the field. Batch 204 at :9443 asks
# whether the ground has been taken or is being stripped, and a county seat can
# be untouched while an empty hillside is occupied. Batch 148 at :5646 and
# :5656 counts WALLS, which `num_buildings` does not count, and a great
# fortress can stand in a province with one economic building on it. `:5874`
# and `:5884` ask which KIND of holding sits here and `:6021` and `:6031` ask
# whether it carries a special building, and none of the three moves with the
# ordinary building count. No gate here is a subset or superset of any of them.
#
# GUARDS. `combat.location` does not always resolve, so both gates carry
# `exists = scope:combat_side.combat.location` as a sibling and repeat it in
# the `trigger_if` limit, exactly as the six band wrappers do from :117 and as
# batch 204 does.
#
# RARITY. A county has one capital and several provinces, so most ground is not
# the seat before the building count is asked at all, and a seat with five
# buildings standing is a worked, prosperous one rather than the usual case.
# Nothing built at all is the other end and is not the usual case either: most
# provinces a war passes through have at least a holding on them. The first
# wants the seat while this side is losing; the second wants the empty ground
# while this side is winning.
bc_they_are_fighting_on_the_county_seat_trigger = {
	exists = scope:combat_side.combat.location
	trigger_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.combat.location }
		scope:combat_side.combat.location = {
			is_county_capital = yes
			num_buildings >= 5
		}
	}
}

bc_there_is_nothing_built_on_this_ground_trigger = {
	exists = scope:combat_side.combat.location
	trigger_if = {
		limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.combat.location }
		scope:combat_side.combat.location = {
			is_county_capital = no
			num_buildings = 0
		}
	}
}
